# 07000014 — the per-placement behaviour audit (level-14-audit)

Every main-map (m54) placement of 07000014 [D], one row each, against the game's own code [M] and
the port's — same method and conventions as docs/levels/0700000d-audit.md. The [D] rows come from
the census probe `census_07000014.rs --ignored` (NF_ISO onfoot image): **1034 placements**, kinds
below; positions and params verbatim. The per-kind game chains were re-verified this session
against work/decomp/ACTION.ELF: creator dispatch `parsemap_create_dynamic_objects` 0x1d04f0 (each
case), the static path `parsemap_block_map_data_static` 0x1d1998 (0x19/0x47 — a runtime cel iff
placement flags carry 0x8000, glist word → cel+0x94 :103; NO dispatcher case for either), and each
create/update/use function cited inline. The 0x2000-flag template placements are skipped by the
dispatcher's param_7 gate [M 0x1d04f0]. GAP column: `none` = no gap found; anything else names the
gap (small gaps fixed this session are marked FIXED; the large ones have report sections in
docs/reports/level-14-audit.md).

Kind-numbering corrections (both [M 0x1d04f0], both already correct in the
port): **0x41 = InverterTrigger_Create 0x1b3040** (class 0x53; the searchlight is 0xde, absent
here) and **0xf9 = SoundTrigger_Create 0x1b2628** (class 0x40) — no 0xF9 inverter rows exist on
this disc (18 rows, all voice lines [D]).

Census-count notes vs docs/levels/07000014.md §1b (this census is [D] over the same disc): 0x29 = 2
(alarmswitchplayer ×2), 0x2e = 3, 0x31 = 11 — the doc's §1b table grouped some rows differently
(0x29 "4", 0x2e "6", 0x31 "5 real + #594"); the per-row tables below are the resolved [D] truth.

The session's earlier unverified list is resolved by this census: 0x29 = 2 rows (both p4 = 1), 0x2e = 3,
0x31 = 11 (#594 the param-less vent prop among them); both 0xdf rows' params read (#425 p0 = 0xff,
#588 p0 = 2); every 0xf5 row carries flags exactly 0xf5; the 0x96 writer stays a live check.


## Kind 0x0f — 61 placements

## Kind 0x0f — 61 placements (characters / drones)
**Game [M]:** case 0x0f → `Drone_Create` 0x136be0 (param_7-gated). Gate
`Drone_bDisableSystem == 0 && p2 (+0x34) <= GameState+0x28 (difficulty)` (:49, u32 <=). Copies pos/rot +
**all 33 param words** (0x84 bytes from tag+0x2C) into the DIVars block (:59) and calls
`NDrone2_CreateObj` (:62); `Rand_Rand(10000)` → obj+0xEC (:70-71). Param meanings pinned by the port's
prior work: p1 = start script (+0x554), p2 = difficulty ceiling, p3 = wait switch channel (+0x134),
p4 = group, p6 = second channel (0x96 = the alarm link on this level), p8 = second tag, p11 = grenade
word, p12 = death channel, p14 = sight class, p23 = alarm patrol. Death channel written by
`DroneFunc_SetDeathChannel` 0x13e660.
**Port:** crates/nf-game/src/drone.rs — `level_drones` (:11226) filters 0x0f, applies the p2 gate
(`param(2) > d.difficulty → None`, citing 0x136be0:49), `Drone::new` maps `switch_channel = param(3)`
(:1917), `second_channel = param(6)`, `second_tag = param(8)`, `death_channel = param(12)` (:1954),
p11 grenade decode with the **07000014-specific clamp** `grenade_weapon → 0x34` (drone.md row). Wait
gating: state entry `if switch_channel != 0 && !channel_on → WAIT_SWITCH` (:6918). Death write:
`set_death_channel` (:4428, raises unless 0/0x63) on the DEATH_ANIM/EXIT arms. Alarm: while ch 0x96,
msg 0x1E to every drone every 30 frames, **once-only on 07000014** (`alarm_sent` latch, :15988).
**GAP (kind-level):** none. (Residual live-checks: who writes ch 0x96 on this level is not pinned in the
doc — script side; and Rook's p13 special-death param is start-state data the port carries per doc §4.)

## Kind 0x29 — 2 placements

## Kind 0x29 — 2 placements per the task's census (the doc's §1b census says 4 — #605/#632
"alarmswitchplayer" → ch 0xDE/0x4F + "2 more"; **count discrepancy unresolved** — the census binary was
not run this session) (Switches)
**Game [M]:** case 0x29 → `Switch_Create` 0x1adcc0 (param_7-gated): class 0x39; inst+0x12 = p0
(**channel**), inst+0x10 = p2 (auto-toggle delay), inst+0x16 = p3 (**rest value**), inst+0x19 byte = p4
(busy tracking), inst+0x14 = p5 (gate); p1 = the **embedded script** (`SP_Create(pos, rot, p1, 0,
mode 8, 0, obj, 0)` :44-45; SP failure deletes the object), p6 ≠ 0 → `SSysItems[p6·8+4] = p0`
(:29-31, snapshot infra). **Create beat: `switch_channels[p0] = p3` + timestamp** (:39-43).
`Switch_Update` 0x1adea8: busy gate (p4 set + count ≠ 0 → return :10-18); value-change arm toggles
obj+0xF4 and **unpauses the embedded SP** (:19-24); auto-return after p2 frames off rest TOGGLES the
channel (0↔1, :25-41) and unpauses. Use: `Switch_Activate` 0x1b02a8.
**Port:** crates/nf-game/src/useobj.rs (`UseObjects::create` 0x29 arm with the full param map :160-181;
`init_channels` rest write; tick: value tracking + latch + SP unpark + auto-return toggle :249-289;
`player_activate` → `Switch_Activate` :292-349); the embedded SP is mounted in
`Drones::attach_level` and bound by placement (drone.rs:12530-12536); scene collects switch scripts for
draw slots (scene.rs:7488-7493).
**GAP (kind-level):** none on this level. (a) The busy gate IS enforced by the port — both
activation paths skip a switch with `busy && latch != 0` (`if s.busy && s.latch != 0 { continue; }`,
useobj.rs:374 and :407; an earlier research note claimed otherwise — wrong). Both m54 switches carry
p4 = 1 [D census #605/#632], so the gate is live here. (b) `SSysItems[p6]` registration is not ported
(save-snapshot infra) — unreachable on m54: both switches carry p6 = 0 [D].

(**open channel**; 0 → `Door_SetupSwing` 0x135440, else the spline slide :72-92), obj+0xF4 = 2 or 4
(use mask from bit 2 of p8|p9 :54-59), obj+0xFD bit 8 = (p4 != 0) (**portal link**), inst+0x76/0x77 =

`Controls_StraddleTest`. Update `Door_Update` 0x1363c8 (lock rising-edge unlock + the "Door Unlocked"
label, level-gated to 0x7000014/0x700004a), `Door_Activate` 0x135be0, `Doors_Calc` 0x135fe0,

## Kind 0xf0 — 32 placements (Pickups)
**Game [M]:** case 0xf0 → `Pickup_Create` 0x18dda0 (param_7-gated): p0 = kind (u16; **6 = the
gold-medal gate `PlrStats_HasGoldMedal`** :93-95; **7..11 → `Pickup_CreateFromSet` 0x18f1b0** :104-105),
p1 = item id (s16; 0xffff → none; the 0x07000008 +0x2C special :116-118), p2/p3 = rounds/type words,
p4 = sound (0 → 0xffff silent), p5/p6 → +0x2C/+0x2E (respawn/lifetime), p7 → +0x34 (the label);
floor-drop + `build_PointOnFloor` when the stack byte is 0; class 0x2F. Update `Pickup_Update`
0x18e148, take `Pickup_Handler` 0x18e650.

model from the glist, sets 7-11 intercept, C-12 no floor drop), take side + inventory; placements

## Kind 0xf5 — 7 placements (AI volumes / shadow volumes)
**Game [M]:** case 0xf5 (and 0xf8) → `Drone_AIVolume_Create` 0x1376c0 (param_7-gated): class 0x3E;
half-extents from the glist bbox × the placement scale triple (:77-84); inst+0x20 = p0 × 0.01 (the
visibility factor), +0x24 u16 = kind map of p1 (0→0, 2→1, 1→2, else 3), +0x28 = p2, byte +0x2C = p3,
+0x26 = p4; **the level-0x0700005 or p1==4 arm shifts the box** (:60-63 — not this level);
LList_Add 0x2706c8. Consumed by `Drone_ProcessOpponents` 0x1390c0 → Drone+0x22C.
**Port:** crates/nf-game/src/aivolume.rs (placement-driven create :33, the scale/factor/kind map),
perception hook in drone.rs (`Drone_ProcessOpponents` visibility scaling).
**GAP (kind-level):** possible over-filter (unverified on m54): the port's filter is
`matches!(p.flags, 0xf5 | 0xf8)` — an EXACT word match — so a placement carrying any extra flag bits
(0x1000 draw bits etc.) would be dropped, where the game only skips the 0x2000 template bit. Every
other kind filter in the port uses `flags & 0xff`. Whether m54's seven 0xf5 words are bare 0xf5 needs
the census run.

## Scripts

Op semantics [M] (all in `work/decomp/ACTION.ELF/`), port side: decode = `crates/nf-formats/src/nis.rs`
(`Nis::parse` + `Stream::decode`), runtime = `crates/nf-game/src/nis.rs` (`Script::run` = `Script_Run` 0x1d8548,
`Script::event` = `Script_EventHandler` 0x1d94c0, `kill` = `Script_KillStream` 0x1d9860).

**[060007c4]** kind-7 NIS, 2240 B, length 280, 12 streams / 34 keys. All entities class 0x31, no parent/spline/params.
- s0/s1/s3: `Wait(0|11) Entity 020007ca|020007c9 → Wait(60|126) EntityEnd End(280)` (s2: same entity, EntityEnd@115).
- s4: 020007c9 (145/215) · s5/s6: 0200021d (19/215) · s7: 0200021a (19/139) · s8: 0200021a (37→241) ·
  s9: 020007df (67→169) · s10: 020007e0 EntityEnd@279 · s11: 0200021d (129→270).
- GAP: none — decodes clean; Wait/Marker/Entity/EntityEnd/End all ported.

**[06000a3b]** 320 B, length 63, 2 streams. s0: Entity 02000750 @0, Entity 02000751 **type 25** params[0,0] @61, End(63).
s1: `Event(kind 0x0c)` @63. GAP: none.

**[06000a1b]** 672 B, length 25, 7 streams. Entities 02000aa2 (s0, 3 params), 02000aa1 (s1), 02000ab3 (s3), 02000aa4 (s5),
all End(25); s2 `Event 0x0f p=[0]`, s6 `Event 0x0f p=[1]` — the SP create-callback hand-off the port cites from this
very script (`nf-game/src/nis.rs:973`: event 0xF wires the streams' ANIM objects). GAP: none.

**[060008af]** 896 B, length 655, 6 streams. s0/s1: Entity 020008ba / 020008bb held to End(655) (markers 90/450/540/560/650).
s2: `Event 0x03 p=[0]` @546 (**p0−1 = −1 → the negative-delay hold**, `Script_EventHandler` 0x1d94c0 case 3) + `Event 0x0c` @652.
s3/s4/s5: `Event 0x0d` @544 — `switch_channels[0x92]=0`, `[0x93]=1`, `[0xb7]=1`. GAP: none.

**[06000886]** 288 B, length 60, 3 streams. s0: Entity 0200089a @0, 0200089b @11, End(60); s1 Event 0x0c @59;
s2 `Event 0x0d [0x8a←1]` @58. GAP: none.

**[06000052]** 11,200 B, length 165, **37 streams / 177 keys** (largest). s0–s2: 0200021a walker streams;
s3/s4: 17 chained `Entity 020003d7…e7` one per tick (256-tick waits = 1 tick apart) with EntityEnd at the tail;
s5: `Light(kind 1)` + LightEnd; s6–s11: 6× short-lived Entity 0200042d (in @0, out @18);

s8: short Entity 0200042d (out @8); s9/18/19/21/22 empty; s23: `Sound id=0x1f6 (502) obj stream 3` + SoundEnd.

**[06000708]** 384 B, length 61, 3 streams. s0: Entity 02000724 with 8 zero params, Entity 02000725 @30, End(61);

**Kind-5 anim scripts** (535, `prefix 06…` names): commands `op0:543 (sequence) op1:113 (sfx) op4:955 (sub-kind)`,
56 looping — exactly `golden/scripts.tsv` (1611 cmds / 543 seqs). Port: `crates/nf-formats/src/anim.rs` `Script::parse`,
runtime `crates/nf-game/src/anim.rs` (`AnimProcessScriptCmds` 0x1c1b38). GAP: none.
Not scripts: kind-6 ×45 = raw sequences; kind-8 `08000001` (27,424 B); kind-16 `woman`.

**Level-wide script GAPs:**
1. **No camera streams**: zero op-0x0d and camera flag = 0 in all 10 headers → `Script_CameraStart` 0x1d9140 and the
   `Camera_PopStates` hand-back are never exercised by 07000014 data (port's camera path rests on synthetic tests).
2. Event kinds used = only 0x03/0x0c/0x0d/0x0f (all ported). Channel writes 0x8a/0x92/0x93/0xb7 are disjoint from the
   mission channels (0x47/0x48/0x4a/0x54/0xce) — cut-scene plumbing, not objective logic.
3. No op 0x0a Anim / 0x1b SubScript / 0x13 Fade / 0x14 Sprite / 0x1e Text anywhere in the level.

## Mission records [L ee_start_05.bin @ EE addr = file offset]

`MissionData` table @0x2a4350 (24 × 40 B); row 20 @**0x2a4670** = `{level 07000014, mission 07000014, part 0,
objectives → 0x2a4260}`. 5 records @0x2a4260 (0x18 B each; `+0x14 state` = 0 pending in the fresh-boot snapshot):

| # | addr | text | name [L USATxt.dat `Lang::bind`] | hint | fail cond | ch | init | en | flags |
|---|----------|----------|--------------------------------------|----------|-----------------|------|---|------|---|
| 0 | 0x2a4260 | 04000049 | "Infiltrate Drake's facility undetected" | 04000050 | 0xffffffff @0x2a4264 | 0x47 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| 1 | 0x2a4278 | 0400004a | "Follow Kiko to the Security Control Center" | 04000051 | 0xffffffff @0x2a427c | 0x48 | 0 | 0x47 | 0 |
| 2 | 0x2a4290 | 0400004b | "Sabotage base security and escape from room" | 04000052 | 0xffffffff @0x2a4294 | 0x4a | 0 | 0x48 | 0 |
| 3 | 0x2a42a8 | 0400004c | "Prevent Rook from cutting the power to Delta Sector" | 04000053 | 0xffffffff @0x2a42ac | 0x54 | 0 | 0x4a | 0 |
| 4 | 0x2a42c0 | 04000069 | "Take the exit to Delta Sector" | 0400006a | 0xffffffff @0x2a42c4 | 0xce | 0 | 0x54 | 0 |

All five names/hints re-bound from `USATxt.dat` (FILES.BIN+3086336) with the port's `Lang::bind` algorithm — strings
match the port's comments verbatim. Enable chain 0→0x47→0x48→0x4a→0x54; no fail labels; flags 0 (no ON-means-fail).

Port: `crates/nf-game/src/mission/c07000014.rs` lines 11–15 — **every field matches** (text/fail/hint/channel/init/
enable/flags; LevelDef level/mission/part; row 20 = `LEVELS[20]`).
**GAP: none on the records.** Observation: record byte +0x0E = 1 exactly on the four objectives that have an enable
channel (0 on obj0) — the port's struct skips it (`init@0x0D` → `enable@0x0F`); behaviorally invisible on this disc
(+0x0E=0 ⇔ enable=0) but unmodeled.

## Game side [M] — script VM (work/decomp/ACTION.ELF/)

- **Dispatcher**: `Script_Run` 0x1d8548 — switch on the op byte (@0x1d8610) per stream cursor
  (`BIN_GetByte` 0x1e8ad8, `BIN_GetWord` 0x1e8af0 = native **LE** u16, `BIN_GetDWord` 0x1e8b10); op 5 = wait-target,
  op 6 = marker (sets SCRIPTINFO+0xab2 bit 1 at length), default = 1-byte skip.
- **Entity streams**: `Script_EntityStart` 0x1d8d78 (hash u32 + parent/spline/type/count bytes + params →
  `Script_CreateEntity` 0x1d8968, obj → stream+0x10, `Script_Interp` 0x1d9a08 poses, `build_LinkToRoom`); `Script_EntityEnd` 0x1dafe8 → KillStream case 1 (re-pose or delete).
- **Anim streams**: `Script_AnimStart` 0x1d8f40 (script/skin/start u32s → `AnimScriptNew` 0x1c1398 family);
  `Script_AnimEnd` 0x1db328 tail-calls `Script_KillStream` (case 2: flag or clear obj+0xe0).
- **Event streams**: `Script_EventHandler` 0x1d94c0 — count byte, kind byte, u32 params; kinds 3…0x13: 3 = rot-copy +
  delay (+0x9c = p0−1, the negative-delay hold), 4 = Player_Disable, 5 = Drone_EnableAll, 6 = matrix copy + flag 0x100,
  7 = flag 0x80, 8 = Drone_CoderCreate, 9 = Player_Cam2Mode, 0xa = Break_DoBreak, 0xb = obj→+0x62 weapon byte,
  0xc = flag 0x10, 0xd = `switch_channels[p0]=p1` (+ timestamp), 0xf = owner create-callback(obj,p0), 0x11 = conditional
  channel write, 0x12 = ResetMap_LevelToLoad, 0x13 = ScriptCam latch. 07000014 fires only 3/0xc/0xd/0xf.
- **Camera streams**: `Script_CameraStart` 0x1d9140 — spline byte + first/last key u16s; guard SCRIPTINFO+0xaa8 bit 0x20;
  Input_ClearAllActions, allocates the CAM record, `Camera_PushStates` 0x1bbc60, enables camera slot 4, SCRIPTINFO+0xab5
  |= 0x10, Music_Event(0xB), letterbox Border sprites (0x2c9468). Per-frame `Script_UpdateCamera` 0x1db518 copies the
  CAM_INFO into the active camera + `Camera_CalcViewAngles`. **07000014 never fires it** (no op 0x0d, camera flag 0).
- **Sound streams**: `Script_SoundStart` 0x1d8820 (id u32 + flat/obj/stream bytes; no obj → `Sound_Play`, else
  `Sound_Play3D`), `Script_SoundEnd` 0x1daf60 → KillStream case 5 `Sound_Stop`.
- **Hand-back**: op 4 → `Script_StreamEnd` 0x1db420 — holds (rewinds −3) while the stream clock is under the script
  length, loops to `loop_start` when SCRIPTINFO+0xab2 bit 3 is set, else clears the run bit and sets done (bit 1).
  The camera hand-back to gameplay is `Script_KillStream` 0x1d9860 **case 4**: `Camera_PopStates` 0x1bbcd0 +
  `Camera_Enable(4, off)` + Mem_Free + Music_Event(0xC) + border sprites off.
